(37b)

\displaystyle=K^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}-I^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.24797pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.24797pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.0699pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.62135pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta j}}}(\hat{\lambda}^{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\hat{X}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}\hat{g}^{\gamma\rho}-\tfrac{1}{2}\hat{X}^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}\hat{h}^{\rho})\,\delta^{u}_{\alpha}.

(37c)

\hat{H}^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}=\hat{F}^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial F^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\partial g^{\gamma\rho}}\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}\hat{g}^{\gamma\rho}-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial F^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\partial h^{\rho}}\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}\hat{h}^{\rho}=0.

\displaystyle\hat{H}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}

\displaystyle=\hat{F}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\partial g^{\gamma\rho}}\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}\hat{g}^{\gamma\rho}-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\partial h^{\rho}}\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}\hat{h}^{\rho}

(37d)

\displaystyle=2\eta_{\alpha u}I^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.24797pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.24797pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.0699pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.62135pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta j}}}(\hat{\phi}^{uj}-\tfrac{1}{2}\hat{X}^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}\hat{g}^{\gamma\rho}-\tfrac{1}{2}\hat{X}^{{{uj}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.44162pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.44162pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.10338pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.64526pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}\hat{h}^{\rho})=-2\eta_{\alpha u}\hat{H}^{{{ui}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.55734pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.55734pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.49315pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}.

These expressions can be further shortened if these objects are defined:

(38a)

\displaystyle\bar{\lambda}^{j}=\hat{\lambda}^{j}-\tfrac{1}{2}\hat{X}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}\hat{g}^{\gamma\rho}-\tfrac{1}{2}\hat{X}^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}\hat{h}^{\rho}

(38b)

\displaystyle\bar{\phi}^{uj}=\hat{\phi}^{uj}-\tfrac{1}{2}\hat{X}^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}\hat{g}^{\gamma\rho}-\tfrac{1}{2}\hat{X}^{{{uj}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.44162pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.44162pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.10338pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.64526pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}\hat{h}^{\rho}.

Hence, if Eq. (
38
) is replaced into Eq. (
37
), the entries of
\hat{\mathbf{H}}
become

(39a)

\displaystyle\hat{H}^{{{ui}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.55734pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.55734pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.49315pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}=-I^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.24797pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.24797pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.0699pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.62135pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta j}}}\bar{\phi}^{uj}

(39b)

\displaystyle\hat{H}^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}=K^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}-I^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.24797pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.24797pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.0699pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.62135pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta j}}}\bar{\lambda}^{j}\delta^{u}_{\alpha}

(39c)

\displaystyle\hat{H}^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}=0

(39d)

\displaystyle\hat{H}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}=-2\eta_{\alpha u}\hat{H}^{{{ui}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.55734pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.55734pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.49315pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}.

Some useful remarks are in order.

Remark 11
.

The correspondence between tensor components and matrix entries in Eq. (
39
) is the same as that of
\hat{F}
(i.e., Remark
10
with
\hat{F}
substituted for
\hat{H}
).

Remark 12
.

From a computational standpoint, Eq. (
38
) is best written in matrix form as

(40)

\bar{\mathbf{x}}=\big\{\bar{x}^{n}\big\}=\begin{Bmatrix}\bar{\lambda}^{j}\\[4.30554pt]
\bar{\phi}^{uj}\end{Bmatrix}=\hat{\mathbf{x}}-\tfrac{1}{2}\hat{\mathbf{X}}\hat{\mathbf{f}},

where
u\in\{1,2,\ldots,R\}
,
j\in\{0,1,\ldots,P\}
,

(41)

\hat{\mathbf{x}}=\big\{\hat{x}^{n}\big\}=\begin{Bmatrix}\hat{\lambda}^{j}\\[4.30554pt]
\hat{\phi}^{uj}\end{Bmatrix}\quad\text{and}\quad\hat{\mathbf{f}}=\big\{\hat{f}^{n}\big\}=\begin{Bmatrix}\hat{g}^{uj}\\[4.30554pt]
\hat{h}^{j}\end{Bmatrix}.

Remark 13
.

The correspondence between vector components and vector entries in Eq. (
41
) is as follows:
\hat{\lambda}^{j}
corresponds to the
(j+1)
-th entry of
\hat{\mathbf{x}}
,
\hat{\phi}^{uj}
corresponds to the
(u+Rj+P+1)
-th entry of
\hat{\mathbf{x}}
,
\hat{g}^{uj}
corresponds to the
(u+Rj)
-th entry of
\hat{\mathbf{f}}
, and
\hat{h}^{j}
corresponds to the
(j+R(P+1)+1)
-th entry of
\hat{\mathbf{f}}
.

Remark 14
.

It is noted that when Eq. (
39
) is compared to Eq. (
33
), one gets:

(42)

\hat{H}^{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}=H^{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}(\hat{x})=F^{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}(\bar{x}).

In other terms,
\hat{\mathbf{H}}=\bar{\mathbf{F}}
.

6.
Error analysis of proposed method

In this section, all indices range over
\{1,2,\ldots,Q\}
, unless otherwise noted.
If any indices are repeated within a term, summation over the repeated index is implied.

Let
y
be a root of
f
, as defined by Eq. (
20
).
Then
f(y)=0
.
If
f
is three times continuously differentiable at
\hat{x}
, and
y
is sufficiently close to
\hat{x}
, then Taylor’s theorem implies that there exist points
\xi
and
\eta
within the region bounded by
\hat{x}
and
y
(two diametrically opposite points defining the opposite vertices of a hyperrectangle), such that:

(43a)

f^{n}(\hat{x})+\frac{\partial f^{n}}{\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\hat{x}}(y^{m}-\hat{x}^{m})+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{n}}{\partial x^{r}\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\hat{x}}(y^{r}-\hat{x}^{r})(y^{m}-\hat{x}^{m})\\
+\frac{1}{6}\frac{\partial^{3}f^{n}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\xi}(y^{a}-\hat{x}^{a})(y^{b}-\hat{x}^{b})(y^{m}-\hat{x}^{m})=0

and

(43b)

f^{s}(\hat{x})+\frac{\partial f^{s}}{\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\hat{x}}(y^{m}-\hat{x}^{m})+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{s}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}}\bigg|_{\eta}(y^{a}-\hat{x}^{a})(y^{b}-\hat{x}^{b})=0.

Multiplying both sides of Eq. (
43b
) by

\frac{\partial x^{r}}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}

and simplifying produces

(44)

\frac{\partial x^{r}}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}f^{s}(\hat{x})+y^{r}-\hat{x}^{r}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial x^{r}}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{s}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}}\bigg|_{\eta}(y^{a}-\hat{x}^{a})(y^{b}-\hat{x}^{b})=0.

Thus, solving for
y^{r}-\hat{x}^{r}
in Eq. (
44
) yields

(45)

y^{r}-\hat{x}^{r}=-\frac{\partial x^{r}}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}f^{s}(\hat{x})-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial x^{r}}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{s}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}}\bigg|_{\eta}(y^{a}-\hat{x}^{a})(y^{b}-\hat{x}^{b}).

Moreover, Eq. (
43a
) can also be expressed as

(46)

f^{n}(\hat{x})+\bigg[\frac{\partial f^{n}}{\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\hat{x}}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{n}}{\partial x^{r}\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\hat{x}}(y^{r}-\hat{x}^{r})\bigg](y^{m}-\hat{x}^{m})+\frac{1}{6}\frac{\partial^{3}f^{n}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\xi}(y^{a}-\hat{x}^{a})(y^{b}-\hat{x}^{b})(y^{m}-\hat{x}^{m})=0.

Inserting Eq. (
45
) into the brackets of Eq. (
46
) and rearranging terms gives

(47)

f^{n}(\hat{x})+\bigg[\frac{\partial f^{n}}{\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\hat{x}}-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial x^{r}}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{n}}{\partial x^{r}\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\hat{x}}f^{s}(\hat{x})\bigg](y^{m}-\hat{x}^{m})\\
-\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial x^{r}}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{n}}{\partial x^{r}\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\hat{x}}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{s}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}}\bigg|_{\eta}(y^{a}-\hat{x}^{a})(y^{b}-\hat{x}^{b})(y^{m}-\hat{x}^{m})\\
+\frac{1}{6}\frac{\partial^{3}f^{n}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\xi}(y^{a}-\hat{x}^{a})(y^{b}-\hat{x}^{b})(y^{m}-\hat{x}^{m})=0.

Reindexing, simplifying and rearranging terms (one more time) yields

(48)

f^{n}(\hat{x})+\bigg[\frac{\partial f^{n}}{\partial x^{m}}\bigg|_{\hat{x}}-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg(\frac{\partial f^{n}}{\partial x^{m}}\bigg)\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}f^{s}(\hat{x})\bigg](y^{m}-\hat{x}^{m})=\\
\bigg[\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{s}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}}\bigg|_{\eta}\frac{\partial}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg(\frac{\partial f^{n}}{\partial x^{c}}\bigg)\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}-\frac{1}{6}\frac{\partial^{3}f^{n}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}\partial x^{c}}\bigg|_{\xi}\bigg](y^{a}-\hat{x}^{a})(y^{b}-\hat{x}^{b})(y^{c}-\hat{x}^{c}),

where the left-hand side of the equation is nothing but:
f^{n}(\hat{x})+\hat{H}^{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}(y^{m}-\hat{x}^{m})
.

Subtracting Eq. (
29
) from Eq. (
48
) and then solving for
y^{m}-x^{m}
gives

(49)

y^{m}-x^{m}=\hat{L}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}\bigg[\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{s}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}}\bigg|_{\eta}\frac{\partial}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg(\frac{\partial f^{n}}{\partial x^{c}}\bigg)\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}-\frac{1}{6}\frac{\partial^{3}f^{n}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}\partial x^{c}}\bigg|_{\xi}\bigg](y^{a}-\hat{x}^{a})(y^{b}-\hat{x}^{b})(y^{c}-\hat{x}^{c}).

Thus, as
\hat{x}\to y
, also
\xi,\eta\to y
and

(50)

\hat{L}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}\bigg[\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{s}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}}\bigg|_{\eta}\frac{\partial}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg(\frac{\partial f^{n}}{\partial x^{c}}\bigg)\bigg|_{f(\hat{x})}-\frac{1}{6}\frac{\partial^{3}f^{n}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}\partial x^{c}}\bigg|_{\xi}\bigg]\\
\to\check{E}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{abc}}}:=\check{L}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}\bigg[\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial^{2}f^{s}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}}\bigg|_{y}\frac{\partial}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg(\frac{\partial f^{n}}{\partial x^{c}}\bigg)\bigg|_{f(y)}-\frac{1}{6}\frac{\partial^{3}f^{n}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}\partial x^{c}}\bigg|_{y}\bigg],

where
\check{L}
is the inverse of
H
at
y
(provided is non-singular).

Substituting the converging constant
\check{E}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{abc}}}
into Eq. (
49
) gives

(51)

y^{m}-x^{m}\sim\check{E}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{abc}}}(y^{a}-\hat{x}^{a})(y^{b}-\hat{x}^{b})(y^{c}-\hat{x}^{c}).

Therefore, for the eigenvalue problem at hand, the following conclusions are noted.
First, since the eigenvalue problem is inherently quadratic, it is clear that

(52)

\frac{\partial^{3}f^{n}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}\partial x^{c}}\bigg|_{y}=0.

Second, the expression:

(53)

\frac{\partial}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg(\frac{\partial f^{n}}{\partial x^{c}}\bigg)\bigg|_{f(y)}\equiv\frac{\partial F^{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg|_{f(y)}

is the same as the one presented earlier in Eq. (
35
).
Third, the expansion of

(54)

\frac{\partial^{2}f^{s}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}}\bigg|_{y}\equiv\frac{\partial F^{{{s}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29686pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64063pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}{\partial x^{a}}\bigg|_{y}

yields eight objects, whose components are formally derived in Appendix B.

Thus, upon expanding

(55)

\check{D}^{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{abc}}}:=\frac{\partial^{2}f^{s}}{\partial x^{a}\partial x^{b}}\bigg|_{y}\frac{\partial}{\partial f^{s}}\bigg(\frac{\partial f^{n}}{\partial x^{c}}\bigg)\bigg|_{f(y)},

it gives rise to 16 distinct objects.
The derivation of each individual component is provided in Appendix C.

Moreover, let
\Delta x^{n}=y^{n}-\hat{x}^{n}
.
Then, from Eqs. (
50
), (
51
) and (
55
), we obtain:

\displaystyle y^{m}-x^{m}

\displaystyle\sim\check{E}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{abc}}}\Delta x^{a}\Delta x^{b}\Delta x^{c}=\tfrac{1}{4}\check{L}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}\check{D}^{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{abc}}}\Delta x^{a}\Delta x^{b}\Delta x^{c}

(56)

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{n\in(vj,j)}\sum_{a\in(\rho,\gamma\rho)}\sum_{b\in(\beta,\alpha\beta)}\sum_{c\in(\sigma,\omega\sigma)}\check{L}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}\check{D}^{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{abc}}}\Delta x^{a}\Delta x^{b}\Delta x^{c}.

Thus, substituting Eq. (

+\check{A}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[46.50447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[46.50447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[28.25957pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[20.18546pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(\gamma\rho)(\alpha\beta)(\omega\sigma)}}}\Delta\phi^{\gamma\rho}\Delta\phi^{\alpha\beta}\Delta\phi^{\omega\sigma},

\check{A}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[36.05392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[36.05392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[21.9116pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[15.65117pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho(\alpha\beta)(\omega\sigma)}}}=-\tfrac{1}{2}I^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.6834pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.6834pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.30493pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.78923pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta\rho}}}I^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.05411pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.05411pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50513pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.93222pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma k}}}\eta_{\alpha\omega}\check{L}^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[14.21478pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.21478pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.66037pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.18599pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(vj)}}}\check{X}^{{{vk}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.67136pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.67136pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.25613pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75438pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}},
